var language = $('#language').val();
function formatCounter(x) {
    return x.toString().replace(/\B(?=(\d{3})+(?!\d))/g, ",");
}

function isEmptyOrSpaces(str) {
    return str === null || str.match(/^ *$/) !== null;
}

    $('.game-checkbox').prop('checked', val);
}

    $('#trialModal h3').text(headerText);

}

function showNotification(message, icon="success", timer=2000) {
    Swal.fire({
        icon: icon,
        title: message,
        showConfirmButton: false,
        timer: timer
    });
}

    $.ajaxSetup({
        headers: {
            'X-CSRF-TOKEN': $('meta[name="csrf-token"]').attr('content')
        }
    });

    // Handle Lock Settings feature
    var message = localStorage.getItem('message');
    if (message) {
        // Display the status message
        $(".my-settings-messages").append(`
          ${message}
          `);
        // Remove the status message from local storage
        localStorage.removeItem('message');
    }
    $('.btn-unlock-settings, .btn-lock-settings').on('click', toggleLockSettings);
    $('.btn-logout-all-devices').on('click', handleLogoutAllDevices);
});

window.toggleLikeRequest = function() {
    if (wait)
        return;

    wait = true;

    setTimeout(function() {
        wait = false;
    }, 750);
    var game_id = $(this).data('game-id');
    var c = '#like' + game_id;

    $.ajax({
        type: 'POST',
        url: '/toggleLike',
        data: {
            game_id
        },
        success: function(data) {
            if (data.like_game === true) {
                $(c).removeClass('fa-heart-o flash');
                $(c).addClass('fa-heart rubberBand');
                $(c).addClass('like-game');
                $(c).tooltip('hide').attr('data-original-title', 'Unlike game').tooltip('fixTitle');
            } else if (data.unlike_game === true) {
                $(c).removeClass('fa-heart rubberBand');
                $(c).addClass('fa-heart-o flash');
                $(c).removeClass('like-game');
                $(c).tooltip('hide').attr('data-original-title', 'Like game').tooltip('fixTitle');
            } else if (data.success === false) {
                $('#trialModal h3').text(data.message);
                $('#trialModal').modal('show');
            }
        }
    });
}

$('i.toggleLike, button.toggleLike').on('click', toggleLikeRequest);

window.toggleFollowRequest = function() {
    if (wait)
        return;

    wait = true;

    setTimeout(function() {
        wait = false;
    }, 750);
    var user_id = $(this).data('user-id');
    var c = '#follow' + user_id;
    var u = '#follow' + user_id + 'user';
    var d = parseInt($(c + '-count2').text());
    var t = $(this);

    $.ajax({
        type: 'POST',
        url: '/user/toggleFollow',
        data: {
            user_id
        },
        success: function(data) {
            if (data.follow_user === true) {
                $(c + '-count').text(formatCounter(d + 1));
                $(c + '-count2').text(d + 1);
                $(c).removeClass('fa-plus-circle flash');
                $(u).removeClass('flash');
                $(c).addClass('fa-minus-circle rubberBand');
                $(u).addClass('rubberBand');
                if ($(t).hasClass('game-menu-follow')) {
                    $(t).removeClass('game-menu-follow');
                    $(t).addClass('game-menu-following');
                    $(t).find('.fa-plus-circle').addClass('fa-minus-circle').removeClass('fa-plus-circle')
                }
                if ($(t).hasClass('profile-menu-follow')) {
                    $(t).removeClass('profile-menu-follow');
                    $(t).addClass('profile-menu-following');
                    $(t).find('.fa-minus-circle').addClass('fa-plus-circle').removeClass('fa-minus-circle')
                }
                $(t).tooltip('hide').attr('data-original-title', 'Unfollow').tooltip('fixTitle');
            } else if (data.unfollow_user === true) {
                $(c + '-count').text(formatCounter(d - 1));
                $(c + '-count2').text(d - 1);
                $(c).removeClass('fa-minus-circle rubberBand');
                $(u).removeClass('rubberBand');
                $(c).addClass('fa-plus-circle flash');
                $(u).addClass('flash');
                if ($(t).hasClass('game-menu-following')) {
                    $(t).removeClass('game-menu-following');
                    $(t).addClass('game-menu-follow');
                }
                if ($(t).hasClass('profile-menu-following')) {
                    $(t).removeClass('profile-menu-following');
                    $(t).addClass('profile-menu-follow');
                }
                $(t).tooltip('hide').attr('data-original-title', 'Follow').tooltip('fixTitle');
            } else if (data.success === false) {
                alert(data.message);
            }
        },
        error: function(xhr, status, error) {
            Swal.fire({
                icon: 'error',
                title: error || "Error",
                text: xhr.responseJSON.message
            });
        }
    });
}

$('i.toggleFollow, button.toggleFollow, div.toggleFollow, span.toggleFollow').on('click', toggleFollowRequest);

window.toggleFolderRequest = function() {
    if (wait)
        return;

    wait = true;

    setTimeout(function() {
        wait = false;
    }, 750);
    var game_id = $(this).data('game-id');
    var folder_id = $(this).data('folder-id');
    var c = '#toggle' + game_id;
    $.ajax({
        type: 'POST',
        url: '/toggleFolder',
        data: {
            game_id,
            folder_id
        },
        success: function(data) {
            if (data.attach_game === true) {
                $(c).removeClass('fa-plus-circle flash');
                $(c).addClass('fa-minus-circle rubberBand');
                $(c).addClass('folder-attached');
                $(c).tooltip('hide').attr('data-original-title', 'Remove game').tooltip('fixTitle');
            } else if (data.detach_game === true) {
                $(c).removeClass('fa-minus-circle rubberBand');
                $(c).addClass('fa-plus-circle flash');
                $(c).removeClass('folder-attached');
                $(c).tooltip('hide').attr('data-original-title', 'Add game').tooltip('fixTitle');
            } else if (data.success === false) {
                let message = data.message.replace(/\n/g, '
          ');
                Swal.fire({
                    icon: 'error',
                    title: 'Oops',
                    html: message
                });
            }
        }
    });
}

window.toggleGameHistoryRequest = function() {
    if (wait)
        return;

    wait = true;

    setTimeout(function() {
        wait = false;
    }, 750);
    var game_id = $(this).data('game-id');
    var activity_id = $(this).data('activity-id');
    var c = '#toggleGameHistory' + game_id;
    var action = $(this).data('action');

    $.ajax({
        type: 'POST',
        url: '/toggleGameHistory',
        data: {
            game_id,
            activity_id,
            action
        },
        success: function(data) {
            if (data.save_game === true) {
                $(c).removeClass('fa-plus-circle flash');
                $(c).addClass('fa-minus-circle rubberBand');
                $(c).addClass('folder-attached');
                $(c).tooltip('hide').attr('data-original-title', 'Remove game').tooltip('fixTitle');
                $(c).data('action', 'remove');
            } else if (data.unsave_game === true) {
                $(c).removeClass('fa-minus-circle rubberBand');
                $(c).addClass('fa-plus-circle flash');
                $(c).removeClass('folder-attached');
                $(c).tooltip('hide').attr('data-original-title', 'Add game').tooltip('fixTitle');
                $(c).data('action', 'restore');
            } else if (data.success === false) {
                alert(data.message);
            }
        }
    });
}

$('i.toggleGameHistory').on('click', toggleGameHistoryRequest);

window.permaDeleteGameRequest = function() {
    if (wait)
        return;

    wait = true;

    setTimeout(function() {
        wait = false;
    }, 750);

    var game_id = $(this).data('game-id');
    var c = '#deletedGameItem' + game_id;
    $.ajax({
        type: 'DELETE',
        url: '/permaDeleteGame',
        data: {
            game_id
        },
        success: function(data) {
            if (data.success === true) {
                $(c).hide();
                var $grid = $('.grid').masonry({
                    itemSelector: '.grid-item',
                    columnWidth: '.grid-sizer',
                    percentPosition: true
                });

                $grid.imagesLoaded().progress(function() {
                    $grid.masonry('layout');
                });

                var game_count = parseInt($('.deleted-game-count').data('gameCount')) - 1;
                $('.deleted-game-count').data('gameCount', game_count);
                $('.deleted-game-count').html(game_count + ' game' + (game_count != 1 ? 's' : ''));
            } else {
                alert(data.message);
            }
        }
    });

    return false;
}

$('button.btnPermaDeleteGame').on('click', permaDeleteGameRequest);

window.restoreGameRequest = function() {
    if (wait)
        return;

    wait = true;

    setTimeout(function() {
        wait = false;
    }, 750);

    var game_id = $(this).data('game-id');
    var c = '#deletedGameItem' + game_id;
    $.ajax({
        type: 'PUT',
        url: '/restoreGame/' + game_id,
        success: function(data) {
            if (data.success === true) {
                window.open('/game/' + game_id, '_blank');

                $(c).hide();
                var $grid = $('.grid').masonry({
                    itemSelector: '.grid-item',
                    columnWidth: '.grid-sizer',
                    percentPosition: true
                });

                $grid.imagesLoaded().progress(function() {
                    $grid.masonry('layout');
                });

                var game_count = parseInt($('.deleted-game-count').data('gameCount')) - 1;
                $('.deleted-game-count').data('gameCount', game_count);
                $('.deleted-game-count').html(game_count + ' game' + (game_count != 1 ? 's' : ''));
            } else {
                alert(data.message);
            }
        }
    });
}

$('button.btnRestoreGame').on('click', restoreGameRequest);

window.destroyQuestionRequest = function() {
    var id = $(this).data('id');
    var c = '#card-' + id;
    var b = '#q-delete-' + id;
    $(b).prop('disabled', true);
    $(b).tooltip('hide');

    $.ajax({
        type: 'DELETE',
        url: '/destroyQuestion',
        data: {
            id
        },
        success: function(data) {
            if (data.deleted === true) {
                $(c).hide();
                $('#tally').text(data.tally);
            } else if (data.success === false) {
                $(b).prop('disabled', false);
                alert(data.message);
            }
        }
    });
}

$('button.q-destroy').on('click', destroyQuestionRequest);

window.updateProblemRequest = function(id) {
    var problem = $('#problem-' + id).text();

    $.ajax({
        type: 'PUT',
        url: '/updateProblem',
        data: {
            id,
            problem,
            language
        },
        success: function(data) {
            if (data.updated === true) {
                $('#problem-' + id).text(data.problem);
                $('#q-update-' + id).css('opacity', 1);
                $('#q-update-' + id).addClass('animated tada');

                setTimeout(function() {
                    $('#q-update-' + id).css('opacity', 0);
                    $('#q-update-' + id).removeClass('animated tada');
                }, 500);
            } else {
                alert(data.message);
            }
        }
    });
}

window.updateMultipleSolutionsRequest = function(id) {
    var solutionList = $(`#multiple-solution-${id} .qeditor-solution`);
    //Hanlde data of multiple solutions
    var solutions = [];
    solutionList.each(function(index) {
        if ($(this).text().trim() != '') {
            solutions[index] = $(this).text().trim();
        }
    })
    $.ajax({
        type: 'PUT',
        url: '/updateMultipleSolutions',
        data: {
            id,
            solutions,
            'question-type': 'multi',
            language
        },
        success: function(data) {
            if (data.updated === true) {
                if (data.question_type == 1) {
                    data.solutions.forEach(function(value, i) {
                        $(`#solution${i + 1}-${id}`).text(value);
                    })
                } else {
                    $(`#multiple-solution-${id}`).replaceWith(`
          ${data.solutions}
          `)

                    var solution = $(`#solution-${id}`).text();
                    var card = id;

                    $(`#solution-${id}`).bind('focus', () => {
                        activeCard = id;
                    }
                    )
                    $(`#solution-${id}`).bind('focusout', () => {
                        activeCard = null;

                        if (wait)
                            return;

                        setTimeout(function() {
                            if (activeCard == null || activeCard !== id) {
                                var soln = $(`#solution-${id}`).text().trim();

                                if (soln !== solution) {
                                    if (soln.length > 160) {
                                        // Reset it if null or too long
                                        $(`#solution-${id}`).text(solution);
                                    } else {
                                        solution = soln;
                                        updateSolutionRequest(id);
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                            wait = false;
                        }, 500);
                    }
                    )
                }

                $(`#multiple-solution-${id}`).attr('data-original-multiple-solutions', JSON.stringify(data.solutions));
                $('#q-update-' + id).css('opacity', 1);
                $('#q-update-' + id).addClass('animated tada');

                setTimeout(function() {
                    $('#q-update-' + id).css('opacity', 0);
                    $('#q-update-' + id).removeClass('animated tada');
                }, 500);
            } else {
                alert(data.message);
            }
        }
    });
}

window.toggleLockSettings = function(e) {
    const className = e.target.getAttribute('class');
    const isUnLock = className.includes('btn-unlock-settings');
    const title = `Enter your login password to ${isUnLock ? 'unlock' : 'lock'} your settings`;
    const htmlContent = '';
    const confirmButtonText = ` ${isUnLock ? 'Unlock' : 'Lock'} Settings`;
    const cancelButtonText = ' Cancel';
    showCommonSwal(title, htmlContent, confirmButtonText, cancelButtonText, 'isLockSetting', function() {
        const password_lock_settings = document.getElementById('password_lock_settings').value;
        if (password_lock_settings === '') {
            Swal.fire({
                icon: 'error',
                title: 'Oops' || "Error",
                text: 'Password is required'
            });
            return false;
        }
        $.ajax({
            type: 'PUT',
            url: '/user/toggleLockSettings',
            data: {
                password: password_lock_settings
            },
            success: function(data) {
                if (data.success === true) {
                    window.location.href = 'mysettings';

                    // Store the status message in local storage
                    localStorage.setItem('message', data.message);
                } else {
                    Swal.fire({
                        icon: 'error',
                        title: 'Oops' || "Error",
                        text: data.message
                    })
                }
            },
            error: function(xhr, status, error) {
                Swal.fire({
                    icon: 'error',
                    title: error || "Error",
                    text: xhr.responseJSON.message
                })
            },
        });
    })
}

window.handleLogoutAllDevices = function(e) {
    const title = 'Enter your Login Password';
    const htmlContent = '';
    const confirmButtonText = ' Sign Out All Devices';
    const cancelButtonText = ' Cancel';
    showCommonSwal(title, htmlContent, confirmButtonText, cancelButtonText, 'isLogoutAllDevices', function() {
        const password_logout_all_devices = document.getElementById('password_logout_all_devices').value;
        if (password_logout_all_devices === '') {
            Swal.fire({
                icon: 'error',
                title: 'Oops' || "Error",
                text: 'Password is required'
            });
            return false;
        }
        $.ajax({
            type: 'POST',
            url: '/auth/logoutAllDevices',
            data: {
                password: password_logout_all_devices
            },
            success: function(data) {
                if (data.success === true) {
                    window.location = '/login';
                } else {
                    Swal.fire({
                        icon: 'error',
                        title: 'Oops' || "Error",
                        text: data.message
                    })
                }
            },
            error: function(xhr, status, error) {
                Swal.fire({
                    icon: 'error',
                    title: error || "Error",
                    text: xhr.responseJSON.message
                })
            },
        });
    })
}

window.copyClassPin = function(e) {
    const classPinUrl = e.target.getAttribute('data-class-pin-url');
    if (classPinUrl) {
        navigator.clipboard.writeText(classPinUrl).then(function() {
            showNotification('Class URL copied.');
        }, function() {
            showNotification('Oops! Your browser does not support the Clipboard API. Please manually select and copy the pin link.', 'error');
        })
    } else {
        showNotification('Oops! Your browser does not support the Clipboard API. Please manually select and copy the pin link.', 'error');
    }
}

window.deleteClassPin = function(e) {
    const passcode = e.target.getAttribute('data-class-pass-code');
    if (passcode) {
        $.ajax({
            type: "DELETE",
            url: `/classpin/${passcode}`,
            success: function(response) {
                showNotification(response.message);
                var classPinRow = document.querySelector(`tr[data-class-pass-code="${passcode}"]`);
                if (classPinRow) {
                    classPinRow.parentNode.removeChild(classPinRow);
                    const dataTable = $('#classPinId table tbody tr').length;
                    if (dataTable === 0) {
                        $('#classPinId').html(`No Class PINs found. Get started by sharing a game.`)
                    }
                }
            },
            error: function(xhr, status, error) {
                console.log(xhr.responseJSON.message);
            }
        });
    }
}

window.extendClassPin = function(e) {
    const game_id = e.target.getAttribute('data-game-id');
    const passcode = e.target.getAttribute('data-class-pass-code');
    if (game_id) {
        $.ajax({
            type: "POST",
            url: `/classpin`,
            data: {
                game_id
            },
            success: function(response) {
                if (response.success) {
                    var date = new Date(response.classPin.expires_at);
                    var options = {
                        month: 'short',
                        day: '2-digit'
                    };
                    var formattedDate = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-US',options).format(date);
                    $(`.class-pin-expire-${response.classPin.passcode}`).text(formattedDate);
                    showNotification(response.message);
                    $(`.btn-extend-class-pin-${passcode}`).removeClass('hidden');
                } else {
                    showNotification(response.message, 'error');
                    $(`.btn-extend-class-pin-${passcode}`).addClass('hidden');
                }
            },
            error: function(xhr, status, error) {
                alert(xhr.responseJSON.message);
            }
        });
    }
}

$('div[contenteditable]').keydown(function(e) {
    if (e.keyCode === 13) {
        e.preventDefault();
    }
});

// polyfill for Object.entries
if (!Object.entries) {
    Object.entries = function(obj) {
        var ownProps = Object.keys(obj)
          , i = ownProps.length
          , resArray = new Array(i);
        // preallocate the Array
        while (i--)
            resArray[i] = [ownProps[i], obj[ownProps[i]]];

        return resArray;
    }
    ;
}

function setWithExpiry(key, value, ttl) {
    const now = new Date()

    // `item` is an object which contains the original value
    // as well as the time when it's supposed to expire
    const item = {
        value: value,
        expiry: now.getTime() + ttl,
    }
    localStorage.setItem(key, JSON.stringify(item))
}

function getWithExpiry(key) {
    const itemStr = localStorage.getItem(key)
    // if the item doesn't exist, return null
    if (!itemStr) {
        return null
    }
    const item = JSON.parse(itemStr)
    const now = new Date()
    // compare the expiry time of the item with the current time
    if (now.getTime() > item.expiry) {
        // If the item is expired, delete the item from storage
        // and return null
        localStorage.removeItem(key)
        return null
    }
    return item.value
}

function showCommonSwal(title, htmlContent, confirmButtonText=' Confirm', cancelButtonText=' Cancel', isCustomClass='', callback) {
    const customPopup = isCustomClass === 'isBatchEditor' ? 'swal-height' : isCustomClass === 'isPauseSubscription' ? 'custom-pause-subscription' : '';

    Swal.fire({
        title: title,
        html: htmlContent,
        showCancelButton: true,
        confirmButtonColor: '#0A91AB',
        cancelButtonColor: '#8D98A2',
        confirmButtonText: confirmButtonText,
        cancelButtonText: cancelButtonText,
        allowOutsideClick: false,
        willOpen: () => {
            if (customPopup === 'swal-height') {
                Swal.getContent().style.maxHeight = '400px';
                Swal.getContent().style.height = '350px';
                Swal.getContent().style.overflowY = 'auto';
            }
        }
        ,
        customClass: {
            popup: customPopup,
            actions: 'right-button-actions-modal'
        }
    }).then( (result) => {
        if (result.isConfirmed) {
            callback();
        }
    }
    );
}

// State-changing team actions must not be GET requests (CSRF), so submit a
// form carrying the page's CSRF token; the server redirects back with a flash.
function submitTeamAction(url, method) {
    var form = document.createElement('form');
    form.method = 'POST';
    form.action = url;
    var fields = {
        _token: $('meta[name="csrf-token"]').attr('content')
    };
    if (method !== 'POST') {
        fields._method = method;
    }
    Object.keys(fields).forEach(function(name) {
        var input = document.createElement('input');
        input.type = 'hidden';
        input.name = name;
        input.value = fields[name];
        form.appendChild(input);
    });
    document.body.appendChild(form);
    form.submit();
}

function handleAction(action, teamId, userId, inviteId) {
    if (!action)
        return;
    if (action === 'makeAdmin') {
        submitTeamAction(teamId + "/toggleRole/" + userId, 'POST');
    } else if (action === 'removeUser') {
        if (confirm("Are you sure you want to remove?")) {
            submitTeamAction(teamId + "/removeUser/" + userId, 'DELETE');
        }
    } else if (action === 'resendInvite') {
        submitTeamAction(teamId + "/resendInvite/" + inviteId, 'POST');
    } else if (action === 'deleteInvite') {
        $.ajax({
            type: 'DELETE',
            url: `${teamId}/invite/${inviteId}`,
            success: function(response) {
                if (response.success) {
                    var inviteRow = document.querySelector(`tr[data-invite-id="${response.team_invitation_id}"]`);
                    if (inviteRow) {
                        inviteRow.parentNode.removeChild(inviteRow);
                        const dataTable = $('#invitationId table tbody tr').length;
                        if (dataTable === 0) {
                            $('#invitationId').html(`
          Empty
          `)
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        });
    }
}

function showErrorCommonSwal(title='Oops', text, confirmButtonText='OK') {
    Swal.fire({
        icon: 'error',
        title: title,

/**
 * Simple Drag & Drop
 * @Version: 1.0.1
 * @Author: Patrick Springstubbe
 * @Contact: @JediNobleclem
 * @Website: springstubbe.us
 * @Source: https://github.com/nobleclem/jQuery-DragNDrop
 *
 * Usage:
 *     $('ul').dragndrop();
 *     $('ul').dragndrop('unload');
 *     $('ul').dragndrop('reload');
 *     $('ul').dragndrop({
 *         onDrop: function( element, droppedElement ){}
 *     });
 *
 **/
(function($) {
    var defaults = {
        loadStyles: true,
        // load default Drag & Drop Styles

        // Callbacks
        onDrop: function(element, droppedElement) {}
    };

    var stylesLoaded = false;
    var stylesDefaults = $('');

    /* Add Plugin to jQuery */
    $.fn.dragndrop = function(options) {
        if (!this.length) {
            return;
        }

        var args = arguments;
        var ret;

        // initialize drag and drop on element(s)
        if ((options === undefined) || (typeof options === 'object')) {
            return this.each(function() {
                if (!$.data(this, 'plugin_dragndrop')) {
                    $.data(this, 'plugin_dragndrop', new DragNDrop(this,options));
                }
            });
            // execute built in method (unload, reload, etc) on element(s)
        } else if ((typeof options === 'string') && (options[0] !== '_') && (options !== 'init')) {
            this.each(function() {
                var instance = $.data(this, 'plugin_dragndrop');

                if ((instance instanceof DragNDrop) && (typeof instance[options] === 'function')) {
                    ret = instance[options].apply(instance, Array.prototype.slice.call(args, 1));
                }
            });

            return ret;
        }
    }
    ;

    /* Setup Functionality */
    function DragNDrop(element, options) {
        this.element = element;
        this.options = $.extend(true, {}, defaults, options);
        this.dragElem = null;

        if (!stylesLoaded) {
            stylesLoaded = true;

            if ($('head > link:eq(0)').length) {
                stylesDefaults.insertBefore($('head > link:eq(0)'));
            } else if ($('head > style:eq(0)').length) {
                stylesDefaults.insertBefore($('head > style:eq(0)'));
            } else {
                $('head').append(stylesDefaults);
            }
        }

        this.load();
    }

    DragNDrop.prototype = {
        load: function() {
            var instance = this;
            var items = $(instance.element).find('> *');

            // make sure this has not already been loaded
            if ($(instance.element).data('plugin_dragndrop')) {
                return true;
            }

            $(instance.element).addClass('jqdndLoaded');

            items.attr('draggable', true).each(function() {
                $(this).on('dragstart', function(event) {
                    instance.dragElem = this;

                    event.originalEvent.dataTransfer.effectAllowed = 'move';
                    event.originalEvent.dataTransfer.setData('text/plain', '');
                    $(this).addClass('jqdndDragging');
                }).on('dragenter', function(event) {}).on('dragover', function(event) {
                    event.preventDefault();
                    event.originalEvent.dataTransfer.dropEffect = 'move';

                    if (this == instance.dragElem) {
                        return;
                    } else if ($(this).prevAll().filter(instance.dragElem).length) {
                        $(instance.dragElem).insertAfter($(this));
                    } else {
                        $(instance.dragElem).insertBefore($(this));
                    }

                    return false;
                }).on('dragleave', function(event) {}).on('drop', function(event) {
                    event.preventDefault();

                    if (typeof instance.options.onDrop == 'function') {
                        instance.options.onDrop(instance.element, instance.dragElem);
                    }
                }).on('dragend', function(event) {
                    event.preventDefault();
                    $(this).removeClass('jqdndDragging');

                    instance.dragElem = null;
                })
            });
        },

        unload: function() {
            var instance = this;
            var items = $(instance.element).find('> *');

            $(instance.element).removeClass('jqdndLoaded');

            items.removeAttr('draggable').each(function() {
                $(this).off('dragstart').off('dragenter').off('dragover').off('dragleave').off('drop').off('dragend');
            });

            $(instance.element).data('plugin_dragndrop', null);
        },

        reload: function() {
            console.log('reload')
            this.unload();
            this.load();
        },

        reset: function() {}
    };
}(jQuery));
